Use BEFORE drafting any plan (ideation direction, experiment Goal anchor, rebuttal strategy, pipeline routing) to expose hidden assumptions, lock topology, and converge on scope. Triggers on: '帮我厘清需求', '我有个想法不太明确', '在写计划前先聊清楚', '深度访谈', 'clarify before planning', 'deep interview', 'requirements interview', 'scope before plan'.
